sufficient hypothesis (smallness, a background inequality), it says nothing about existence. Only the point-test and
weak-probe entries say "no exact solution in this ball", and only for the stated ball at the reference c. For state

pinning") extends each such entry to every c in the same weighted affine chart.

For C1-N1-007 the reasons are its CONFIRMED fixed-c L^2 distance 0.004769166867 (T4-185 with T4-190 and N4-157;
every scalar ball and every graded and gradedL2 box; with its CONFIRMED nonzero leading profile, N4-165 reproducing

at every c) and point value (N4-091 with T4-108; every scalar ball) and a one-point bound uniform over every original graded and gradedL2 box (N4-102,
CONFIRMED by T4-122). In the G0-dominated weighted norms of the matrix 007 is the best candidate so far by FLOAT values (G0 0.3767; whole-plane
G0 = 0.37668932 by the N3 numerics worker, CONFIRMED by N4-139; ||F||_H 0.6355), although its G1 3.43910379 and P2 190.8266911 (the N3 numerics worker, CONFIRMED by N4-153) exceed those of d2 (1.029, 20.54). It is the first exactly

    2.137754e-6 (s = 1/52441). K_L = 2 corresponds to (L) as stated. The same norm with weights (2^14, 2^18), so the
    elementary bound applies.
  - T1 TP-1 (CONFIRMED by T4-059): the time-dependent bound (L) may be replaced by the stationary low hypothesis (L0):
    [0, infinity) lies in the resolvent set of A_k with ||A_k^-1|| <= K_L uniformly on B; it transfers to a high
    inverse. The thresholds as functions of K_L are above (T4-067). TP-1b (reduction of (L0) to finite-rank
    certificates): The T1/T3 theory worker's revised version, which bounds the finite-rank forcing against raw L^2, is CONFIRMED under
    its generator and low-resolvent hypotheses (T4-073); the alpha' condition of T4-059 is no longer needed. A
    quantitative Woodbury margin and a K_L certificate for any candidate are OPEN.
- FW-T-DYADIC-Y-v1[a, s, Rw], scale-adapted weighted high space (the T4 theory auditor T4-058; registered in theory_v02 section 3
  with the norm NORM-T-DYADIC-Y-v1 and the lower comparison norm NORM-T-WLAP-v1[a]; AUDITED conditional on its hypotheses (1), (8), (9), (H), (12) and
  F in Y: The T2 theory worker review the review of T4-058, frozen by T4-095): a dyadic density-velocity
  graph norm with globally bounded gradient and a conditional nonlinear closure under a uniform high resolvent
  hypothesis (H), which is OPEN with its constant and forcing bound. It admits a leading r^gamma profile when
  a - 1 < gamma, so it is a candidate setting for C1-BASE-S131 (row 34); no concrete high resolvent estimate (H) is
  proved, so no threshold; rows 58 and 59 record exclusions only. Consequences from the T2 theory worker review, CONFIRMED by T4-096: the closure radius
  satisfies R < R_univ(a) = sqrt(8 pi)(1 + c_l - c_l a)/(1 + a); at a = 221/200 with the certified rate 0.02181481 the
  scheme needs M < 1.2613e-12, and M < 7.4294e-11 for any possible rate at that weight. T4-096 REFUTES two implications
  of the review (an origin energy packet does not force s < c_l - 1 for (H); a lower rate certificate does not bound
  the best rate) and leaves OPEN the review's exclusion of state 131 for every a. For d2, Y(F) >= 0.041637 (the T2 theory worker). T4-097 (AUDITED through the T1/T3 theory worker
  R1-013, T4-114): under its finite-angular decay hypotheses every actual inverse bound (H) has
  M >= 1/(1 + c_l - c_l a), so the scheme needs Y(F) < sqrt(8 pi)(1 + c_l - c_l a)^2/(1 + a). For state 131 the
  complete Laplacian tail beyond r = 8192 (relative remainder < 0.4662, T4-098) and a 256-cell cover of
  2/c_l < a < 1 + 1/c_l (minimum squared ratio > 11.2378, T4-099) violate this; both computations are CONFIRMED (N4-093,
  N4-094, T4-105). The scheme is therefore RULED OUT for state 131 at every a, s and Rw within these hypotheses
  (row 58, T4-114); this is not nonexistence and does not exclude other high spaces. Rounded-up ceilings at a = 1, 6/5,
  13/10, 7/5, 3/2: 2.506629, 0.927368, 0.455033, 0.158974, 0.018038 (T4-114). For d2 the continuous comparison
  Y(F) >= 5^((a-2)/2)||(1+r^2)^((2-a)/2) Delta F||_2 for 0 < a < 2 (T4-107, AUDITED through the T1/T3 theory worker R1-014, T4-120)
  and the CONFIRMED seven-cell comparison (ratio > 1.35446; T4-112, N4-100, T4-116) rule the scheme out for every
  0 < a < 2, 0 < s < 1 and finite Rw (row 59). Other high spaces are not excluded. For C1-N1-007 the first and third
  angular modes exclude it for 4/5 <= a < 2 (T4-127, CONFIRMED by N4-113 and reviewed by the T1/T3 theory worker R1-015; row 64), and the origin ceiling of T4-146 with
  the cover of T4-147 excludes it for 0 < a < 4/5 (AUDITED through the T1/T3 theory worker R1-017), so the scheme is RULED OUT for 007
  at every 0 < a < 2, 0 < s < 1 and finite Rw within the stated hypotheses. Spectral form
  (the T1/T3 theory worker R1-013 remark 3, CONFIRMED by T4-124): in the stated closed realization on Y the line
  Re lambda = -(1 + c_l - c_l a) lies in the approximate point spectrum, so every strongly continuous semigroup has
  norm at least exp(-(1 + c_l - c_l a) t), and a transient prefactor cannot give a faster decay rate. The extension
  to compact and finite-rank perturbations (resolvent norm at least 1/|Re z + 1 + c_l - c_l a| wherever it exists) is
  AUDITED through the T2 theory worker's review (T4-131), so a finite-rank repair of the operator on Y does not remove the floor.

Elementary lower bound used below (no lemma needed): the symbol of J^s is at least 1, so by Plancherel
N_s(F) >= (2^14 G1^2 + 2^18 G0^2)^(1/2) and X_s(F) >= (2^14 G1^2 + 2^18 G0^2)^(1/2) for every s >= 0, with
G0 = ||F||_2, G1 = ||grad F||_2. For C1-A2-d2: G0 = 4.98881700456802, G1 = 1.02914187145367 (the N3 numerics worker, CONFIRMED-N4
N4-024, N4-026), so both norms are at least 2557.67. In the same way X_(s,w)(F) >= (w1 G1^2 + w2 G0^2)^(1/2), which
for d2 is at least 2557.67 for w = (2^14, 2^18) and at least 5111.94 for w = (2^15, 2^20). Also P2 = 20.53677 (the N3 numerics worker, CONFIRMED-N4 N4-043) and
Q3 = ||omega D^3 F||_2 = 128.14736 (the N3 numerics worker, CONFIRMED-N4 N4-050).

Structural facts that constrain every future row (A2-THEOREM unless marked):
- Sector conditions (T4-033 CONFIRMED). For NORM-T-X3-v1 (FW-T-T4L2-v1, gradedL2) exactly E_(1,0) = E_(2,0) =
  E_(3,0) = 0 is required; adding the Hdot^-1 component (every N_s framework) requires also (4,0), (5,0), (0,1), (1,1).
  Exact cancellation at every harmonic is needed; small coefficients do not suffice. After n stationary principal
  branches the sector (n,0) is fixed by the lower profiles, full-circle norms E_4 = 345.672, E_5 = 1045.347,
  E_6 = 3081.274, E_7 = 9679.966, E_8 = 35192.42 (CONFIRMED, N4-032, T4-027), E_9 = 628494.74, E_10 = 910751.02,
  E_11 = 3586644.79, E_12 = 10739524.91 (N4-058: exact recursion through order 12 agrees with the N1/N2 numerics worker's table,
  CONFIRMED for that finite table; no convergence statement); E_13 = 54390341.71, E_14 = 143089426.94,
  E_15 = 436638208.10, E_16 = 1106012053.25 (N4-098, exact recursion through order 16, CONFIRMED for the finite
  table). Every finite angular step is invertible through order 16 (1530 exact pivots; T4-123, CONFIRMED by N4-109, whose independence T4-134 checks);
  order 8 has the near-resonant divisor s_8 = -116183978028673/4076476460239181 (about -0.0285) with homogeneous
  first-mode velocity amplification 49.9745 (N4-109, CONFIRMED by T4-134; a local diagnostic, no convergence statement).
- Cost of a fixed sector in the L^2 route (theory_v01 NC-T-TAILCOST): if F equals E r^e exactly on r > T, then
  ||F||^2_(L^2(r > T)) = E^2 T^(2e+2)/(-(2e+2)) exactly. This is a necessary condition only under exact sector equality
  on r > T; a leading coefficient alone gives no lower bound for a full residual (N4-041, T4-036). The onset radii for
  the G0 requirement 1.19e-8 are in row 33. The residual must also be below the thresholds on the whole region inside
  the onset radius.
- Tail cost by onset radius (numerics_v02 R1 and section 3b; the same exact pure-exterior formula with full-circle
  norms E_4 to E_12, which are CONFIRMED). If the residual equals the first surviving sector (n,0) exactly on r > T,
  then ||F||_(L^2(r>T)) is at least 142.9 for every n = 4 to 12 at T = 10; at T = 100 it is 167.4 (n = 4), 3.751
  (n = 7), 1.532 (n = 8), 0.0895 (n = 12); at T = 1000 it is 1.36e-5 for n = 12. The N1/N2 numerics worker's conclusion (planning
  judgment, not a theorem): the binding constraint for the L^2 route is the radius T out to which the inner part
  reproduces the solution, not the number of exact branches. The A2 inner basis (degree 25 in t = r^2/(1+r^2))
  resolves only to r of order 20 (the N1/N2 numerics worker, FLOAT). numerics_v03 section 3b (restated after an the second model referee
  check; a claim, not audited): at T = 1000 the cost is 1.4e-5 at 12 branches and 4.9e-9 at 16, and an isolated sector
  falls below 1e-8 only for onsets above about 6611 (12 branches) or 881 (16 branches), an order-onset trade-off. The N4 numerical auditor
  REFUTES its literal sentence that the T = 10 cost never decreases with the branch count (isolated cost 943.6027 at
  order 9, 448.9448 at order 10) and CONFIRMS the finite costs and the onset radii 6611.42 (order 12) and 880.73 (order
  16) from CONFIRMED inputs (N4-103; its printed exponent is corrected to alpha_n = 1 - (n+1)(1 - 1/c_l) by
  N4-108, numbers unchanged); these are no lower bound for a residual with several sectors. numerics_v04 section 3b still
  carries the refuted sentence (theory_v02 R5 asks the N1/N2 numerics worker to drop it). The
  convergence radius r_c of about 12 for the stationary tail series is HEURISTIC. Exterior Hdot^-1 of a zero-extended
  sector 1_(r>T) r^e sum_k b_k sin(k theta), e < -2: ||.||_H^2 = pi T^(2e+4)/(-2e-4) sum_k b_k^2/(k(k-e-2)) (the N1/N2 numerics worker
  with the referee, not audited).
- Exterior negative-norm tail (T4-014, AUDITED through the T2 theory worker's review, T4-056): an exact exterior sector gives an
  interior-independent lower bound on the Hdot^-1 norm; the minimum over all Hdot^-1 extensions is attained by the
  exterior density plus a negative boundary layer (the T2 theory worker Remark R1, CONFIRMED in T4-056). T4's added shell estimate that L^2
  membership alone does not raise the minimum is AUDITED through the T2 theory worker's review (T4-135), and its order
  epsilon^(1/2) is sharp for a fixed odd mode.
- Truncation (T4-036, CONFIRMED by the N4 numerical auditor): zeroing the descendants q = 1, 2, 3 of a profile truncated at branch N does
  not remove the principal interactions h = N + 2, ..., 2N + 1; those sectors must be checked too.
- Low weights (theory_v01 R3, HEURISTIC argument): a far-field weight r^(-sigma) on the L^2 level is compatible with
  the transport inverse only for sigma < beta0/c_l (about 1.13 for state 131); r^-2 or exponentially localized weights
  are impossible. The only weighted low theorem (T4-022, AUDITED as a conditional low-space theorem, T4-045) needs R = 2^29 and gives no discount below r = 5e8.
  T4-040: the statements that a decaying residual never benefits from weights and that no inverse improvement can
  supply the missing factor are not theorems (A2 P29 gives a smooth S1 function with the fixed tail below the weighted
  threshold, realizability open). They are planning judgments about the present candidates and estimates. The
  statement that H binds for every residual away from the origin is REFUTED as a norm statement (annular
  oscillations cos(Nx) have small Hdot^-1 norm); for d2 the H dominance is a separate numerical observation.
- Theorem D(b) requires ||omega D^2 F||_2 <= 6.4e-7, ||grad F||_2 <= 5.0e-9, ||F||_2 <= 1.24e-9 and ||F||_H <= 1.27e-9
  separately (section 8). FW-T-TB-rebal-v1 relaxes these by 14.4 and FW-T-TB-sharp-v1 by 166; FW-T-T4L2-v1 drops H
  and needs G0 <= 1.19e-8. Every framework in the record has a weight of at least 2^9 on G0 and needs ||F||_2 of order 1e-7 or
  less; the exactly admissible candidates have ||F||_2 between 5 and 312.
- Signed condition (B-L2) of FW-T-T4L2-v1: for the archived refits theta must be below 0.0999 (d2) and 0.0977 (Mpen2);
  the new exact candidates C1-N1-002, 003, 004 have theta at least 47.6, 30.9 and 0.88 at rational witness points
  (N4-046, N4-049, CONFIRMED by T4-046). Failure of this sufficient condition does not disprove coercivity. T4-047
  (AUDITED conditional on its signed-decomposition, bounded-coefficient and canonical-velocity hypotheses through
  the T1/T3 theory worker R1-011, T4-080): a contractive L^2(w^2) energy estimate after any compact additive correction has rate at
  most c + 1 - ess sup[b + (|D rho| - rho_y)/2]; it does not exclude invertibility or a semigroup bound with prefactor
  above 1 (which is what (L) of T4-051 allows). Its seven state 131 witness points are CONFIRMED (N4-063).
  C1-N1-007 is the first exactly admissible candidate where (B-L2) holds: exact kappa_HS = 0.72816468 (N4-092,
  CONFIRMED by T4-111) needs theta <= 0.08215, and a complete 223-cell cover certifies theta <= 2/25 on the whole plane
  (N4-111, CONFIRMED by T4-162's independent 232-cell cover; signed rate > 2.00215, > 2.02568 with T4-161). C1-N1-008 (same basis, no rF term) fails the
  prescribed criterion: exact kappa_HS = 0.84604 > c_l - 1 (N4-107, CONFIRMED by T4-126). C1-N1-009: exact
  full-plane kappa_HS = 0.71321 (N4-121, CONFIRMED by T4-166) needs theta <= 0.09710 (FLOAT theta 0.01129). C1-N1-010 fails in
  FLOAT (kappa 0.922). FW-T-T4L2-bL-v1
  (T4-113) allows a margin beta_L < 2 at a smaller threshold (1.066e-4 at beta_L = 1 against 2.1035e-4).
- Parity budget for the S1 commutator (the T4 theory auditor T4-128; the inequality and the d = 12 radial matrices for C1-N1-008 are
  independently proved and reproduced by the N4 numerical auditor, N4-116, CONFIRMED; the T4 theory auditor checks N4-116 in turn, T4-137): the double commutator preserves the four parity
  blocks, so for an even-even finite family with Gram matrix G and compression B the S1 operator norm is at most
  sqrt(kappa_HS^2 - tr(G^-1 B G^-1 B)); radial Poisson derivatives give exact rational matrices. C1-N1-008: 0.82582 at
  d = 12 (CONFIRMED), 0.81512 at d = 32 (T4-129, CONFIRMED by N4-124), 0.80747 with 80 tests in three parity blocks (T4-132,
  CONFIRMED by N4-119 through an independent Legendre and Fourier construction; the 28-test run of T4-130 gives
  0.82585, also CONFIRMED by N4-119), the first value below c_l - 1, leaving theta <= 0.00285 for rate 2. At d = 12 (T4-129,
  CONFIRMED by N4-133 to N4-135 and accepted in T4-161, so AUDITED arithmetic with R1-016): C1-N1-007 0.70464, C1-N1-005 1.33242, C1-A2-d2 0.68631, so the rate-two theta
  allowances are 0.10568 (007) and 0.12401 (d2), and 005 has none (allowance -0.52210); these are allowances, not
  theta certificates. Using such a bound in place of kappa_HS in (B-L2)
  (the low-generator extension T4-128 (2)) is AUDITED conditional through the T1/T3 theory worker R1-016: its hypotheses are a
  full-plane theta bound and the completeness and Lipschitz bounds of V. These are sharper sufficient bounds, not operator norms; no row
  changes. Signed form (the T4 theory auditor T4-133 with its outward LDL criterion; reviewed as valid by the N4 numerical auditor, N4-118, accepted in
  T4-139, and CONFIRMED by the T1/T3 theory worker, R1-016, so AUDITED): if T_other
  is the captured complementary-parity energy, T_H = tr(G^-1 B G^-1 B) for an S1 compression and q >= 0 satisfies
  qG - B >= 0, then <Kh, h> <= sqrt(kappa_HS^2 - T_other - T_H + q^2) ||h||_2^2. It is an upper bound on the quadratic
  form, not on the operator norm. Union of compressions (the T4 theory auditor T4-138; reviewed as valid by the N4 numerical auditor, N4-120, and in
  T4-142, and CONFIRMED by the T1/T3 theory worker, R1-016, so AUDITED): for commuting orthogonal projections the angular and radial captured energies
  add after the common block is subtracted once. For C1-N1-008, with the d = 32 radial space and the 80 angular tests,
  the absolute S1 bound is 0.79218 and theta <= 0.01814 suffices for rate 2 (T4-141, CONFIRMED by N4-125 with
  the d = 32 matrices of N4-124); with the 32-test signed compression (exact q = 4615759/200000000, positive pivots by 1024-bit outward

  theta <= 0.02414 (T4-143; the N4 numerical auditor reproduces qG - B > 0 by a direct 2048-bit LDL with 32 positive pivots, N4-126, and

- Localized (B-L2) (the T2 theory worker note on the localized (B-L2) margin, 01:52Z; the T4 theory auditor T4-157 to T4-159). In unweighted L^2_S1 the best
  energy rate of A_0 satisfies 1 + c - sup theta - kappa <= beta* <= 1 + c - sup theta (Proposition L1), so an
  unweighted energy rate pays the global supremum of theta. With a bounded transport weight w = exp(psi),
  g = V.grad psi and theta <= theta_c + theta_1, the rate in L^2(w^2) is at least
  ess inf(1 + c + g - theta_1) - theta_c - kappa - C_T ||grad theta_1||_2 - omega, with
  C_T = sqrt((4 log 2 - 1)/(8 pi)) = 0.26557 and omega <= ||grad rho||_inf (exp(osc psi) - 1) (Lemma C and
  Proposition L2, CONFIRMED by T4-157, so AUDITED; semigroup conclusions keep their generator and core conditions).
  The suggested Hilbert-Schmidt bound for the first velocity commutator [U, w^-1] is REFUTED (T4-158). For the orbit
  weight of Lemma L4 the literal everywhere derivative is REFUTED, the needed orbit inequality is CONFIRMED and
  compact support is OPEN (T4-159); Proposition L3 (the necessary price osc psi >= Theta(lambda)) is CONFIRMED by
  T4-168 for bounded locally Lipschitz psi with V.grad psi in L^inf (so AUDITED; the indicator test of the sketch is
  replaced by smooth shrinking bumps): any weighted rate beta forces V.grad psi >= theta - (1 + c - beta) a.e. and
  the matching orbit budget for osc psi. The paragraph after Lemma L4 claiming that the auxiliary theta_1 budget

  bump; the zero weight has rate 1 + c). T4-170 (compact, globally Lipschitz orbit weight under a uniform negative
  exterior drift, an added hypothesis) is CONFIRMED by the T1/T3 theory worker R1-020 (T4-180, so AUDITED), with the sharper support
  radius r2 exp(L_r Theta_1/delta) when Theta_1 is a rigorous upper bound. When Sym DV >= mu I with mu > 0 the
  orbit weight satisfies Lip(psi) <= Lip(theta_1)/mu for every time horizon (the T2 theory worker the review of T4-170 R1, CONFIRMED by

  conjugation error Sym(e^psi Q e^-psi) - Sym Q is Hilbert-Schmidt under bounded Lipschitz grad rho and psi, which
  replaces the conjugation cost omega of L2 by the smaller of the new bound and the old one; no candidate rate follows

- Necessary point test (AUDITED, T4-007 with the T1/T3 theory worker and the T2 theory worker reviews, T4-015): any exact correction h with N_s(h) = r
  satisfies |F(X)| <= A(X) r + B r^2, A(X) = C1 |V(X)| + C0 (1 + |grad rho_bar(X)|), C0 = 1/(2 sqrt(pi (1 + s))),
  C1 = 1/sqrt(8 pi s (1 + s)), B = C0 C1. On the S1 symmetry axis C1 improves to C1/sqrt(2) for |y| >= 1/2 (T4-017,

- Weak necessary test (Proposition W of T2-R3, AUDITED T4-042, no inverse hypothesis): if rho + h solves the
  fixed-c equation with ||h||_2 <= m and ||Dh||_inf <= delta, then |<F, phi>| <= m (||V.grad phi + (2c+1) phi||_2 +

  m = r/512. It is much stronger than the point test for residuals with large ||F||_2: with phi = y/(1+r^2)^2, d2 needs

- Weighted energy obstruction map at state 131 (the T1/T3 theory worker TP-3, CONFIRMED by T4-084): for weighted L^2 energies with weight
  exponent a and scale R, Lambda(a, R) >= a c_l for every R, so the energy rate is at most c_l + 1 - a c_l; there is no
  positive rate for a >= 1.552389 and no rate 2 for a > 0.447611. 58 (a, R) entries (a = 6/5 to 8/5) have a certified

  weights of exponent p < 3 - gamma, every real Bessel order and the unweighted homogeneous order-1 case): for a

- Annular obstruction (N4-055, CONFIRMED by T4-049): under smooth sublinear background hypotheses, a nonzero leading
  residual sector r^q E(theta) with q >= -1 excludes every correction in L^2 with bounded gradient (in particular every
  H^(2+s) correction) of any size, and a changed c changes the leading term, so no c helps. It applies to state 131
  (q = 0.1048) and not to d2 or to candidates whose sectors with q >= -1 vanish. T4-049's version without the gradient
  bound (every L^2 distributional divergence-form correction) is CONFIRMED by N4-062. It does not exclude non-L^2 tail
  repairs, weighted correction classes or a different affine background.
- Weak tail exponent pinning (T4-189, AUDITED: CONFIRMED by the T1/T3 theory worker R1-023 and by the T2 theory worker the review of T4-189, cited in T4-200):
  if rho has a nonzero leading r^gamma angular profile with 0 < gamma < 1 and the stated growth bounds, every
  stationary correction h with h and U[h] in L^2((1+r^2)^(-a) dX), 0 <= a <= 1 + gamma, has exponent c' = 1/gamma; no
  derivative or pointwise asymptotics of h is needed, and the endpoint a = 1 + gamma is included (sharp, since a larger
  a admits h = -rho). T4-201 (CONFIRMED) removes the separate weighted velocity hypothesis and the background gradient
  bound for the canonical continuous extension. For C1-N1-007, T4-203 gives a nonzero leading r^(1/c_l) density profile with first sine
  coefficient between 18 and 19 (rounded 18.5678), CONFIRMED by the N4 numerical auditor's independent summation (N4-165, which also
  checks the uniform finite-branch expansion and U = O(r^gamma)). So every exact solution in 007's affine class whose
  correction lies in a registered unweighted L^2 ball has c = c_l, and the fixed-c distance 0.004769166867 excludes
  such solutions at every c (rows 56, 57). T4-210 (CONFIRMED by the N4 numerical auditor's independent recurrence evaluation N4-172,
  accepted in T4-213) shows that state 131, C1-A2-d2 and C1-N1-005 to C1-N1-012 have the same entire leading density
  profile (every multi-scale term of 010 and 011 vanishes at leading order), so the same pinning holds for each of
  them and every confirmed fixed-c "no exact solution" entry of their rows holds at every c in the same chart. This
  does not exclude solutions in other charts or with another leading tail, and failed sufficient hypotheses
  (smallness, background inequalities) are not affected. T4-199 (AUDITED: The T1/T3 theory worker R1-024, accepted in T4-212): the
  potential-to-density map is injective on the 007 generator span modulo the 1060 zero-potential directions, so the
  density span has dimension 1922; generators with an explicit p, legq or legqs factor (010, 011) need their own
  argument. None of these gives existence.
- Weighted residual-tail obstruction (T4-202, CONFIRMED by the T4 theory auditor from the T2 theory worker's review of T4-189 Remark R3, so AUDITED
  under its hypotheses): for a <= 2, a residual with a uniform nonzero leading power q >= a - 1 excludes every weighted
  L^2 correction of the affine class (q >= max(a - 1, 2a - 3) if a > 2). This is an affine-class obstruction, not a
  contraction failure; an application to a candidate needs its exact nonzero leading coefficient and a uniform
  expansion.
- Tail charts (T4-205, AUDITED: The T1/T3 theory worker R1-025, accepted in T4-220): tails chi r^gamma Theta with gamma >= a - 1 are
  independent modulo L^2((1 + r^2)^(-a)), and no finite linear or log-polynomial augmentation of the correction space
  represents an exponent change. A solution with another exponent therefore needs a new reference chart with
  nonlinear tail coordinates (Route T-E). Fixed-c dilation is different. T4-221 CONFIRMS R1-025 R1 and R3: exponent
  extraction is unique but not continuous, and for 0 <= a <= 2/c_l the dilation orbit is not contained in

  added as a nonlinear coordinate (review set 24). T4-234 (AUDITED: The T1/T3 theory worker R1-030, accepted in T4-246 and

- Annular first-moment adjustment (the T4 theory auditor, T4-359, audit record annular_moment_adjustment, proof sha256 0b3944af...,

  accepted dependency. Statement: a compact mode-1 annular correction h_R with prescribed first moment m has zero
  density and velocity on r < R, exterior velocity m(cos 3theta, sin 3theta)/(pi r^3), L^2 and sharpw high norm O(|m|
  R^-2), H_q cost diverging for q < -3, and changes an endpoint residual only by (m/pi)P. So the present high norm
  controls neither the first moment nor H_q. It is reference design, not an existence result. T4-371
  (audit record moment_interpolation_audit, proof c6651df9..., result a6877651...) CONFIRMS the T2 theory worker's remark R2 as a
  Hoelder interpolation: for q < -3, ||h||_2 <= beta and |h| <= H(1+|X|)^q give |first moment| <= Csplit(d) beta^theta
  H^(1-theta), theta = d/(d+2), d = -q-3 (theta = 0.18287676790316613... at q*), with optimal exponent on bounded H_q
  sets; it CONFIRMS the R3 endpoint minimizing moment and the R4 interior harmonic formulas. A moment is controlled
  only when an L^2 budget and a weighted amplitude budget are both supplied. R3's bump costs remain FLOAT, and the
  step from R3's drift-term comparison to the complete annular residual change for every R is OPEN (the other terms
  can cancel). No candidate or ball exclusion changes.
  T4-373 also reviews the T1/T3 theory worker's R1-037: remarks (a) (no bounded embedding of the sharpw high norm into H_q for any q
  < -1), (b) (the part of the endpoint coefficient orthogonal to P is invariant under compact changes) and (c)
  (||h||_2 >= 2|m_y|/(sqrt(pi) A^2) for support in r <= A, so the power R^-2 is optimal) are CONFIRMED; remark (d) is
  REFUTED: the drift term cannot be dropped, and for fixed m != 0 and gamma < 1 the complete residual change has
  ||F(rho + h_R) - F(rho)||_2 asymptotic to |m| A_c R^-2 with A_c = ||(c X.grad - 1)f||_2 >= (c+1)||f||_2 > 0, not
  O(R^(gamma-3)). This gives no lower bound at a finite radius without background estimates. T4-372
  (audit record sharp_amplitude_moment, proof 6283be5d..., result cd50a52f...) proposes the optimal constant of the
  T4-371 amplitude inequality (Csharp = 9.15513474115363927... at the campaign d, ratio 0.97149 to Csplit); AUDITED
  (the T1/T3 theory worker's R1-039 CONFIRMS it and T4-382 accepts that review); its sharpness holds only in the homogeneous amplitude
  class, not in the weighted C1 class, and it supplies no H_q budget and no change to the T4-351 cap.
- Origin restriction (T4-060, AUDITED conditional on its coefficient hypotheses through the T1/T3 theory worker R1-012, T4-091; the graph
  variant also depends on the T4-058 definitions): for a normalized S1 reference with partial_y V_y(0) = 1, a
  contractive H^q energy estimate on normalized tests needs rate beta <= c + 1 - q; the H^3 energy has limiting
  growth 2 - c > 3/20 in the case 4 range, and a positive H^(2+s) contractive gap needs s < c - 1. It does not exclude
  a transient semigroup bound, a stationary inverse or an adapted equivalent norm. T4-140 (the T4 theory auditor; its bound is the alpha_v case of
  T4-145 and is AUDITED with it through the T1/T3 theory worker R1-017) addresses the transient case: in an H^q realization satisfying its smooth-domain and
  generator hypotheses, ||S(t)|| >= exp((q - c - 1) t) even with bounded finite-rank pinning and compact
  perturbations, so a bound C exp(-beta t) with finite C needs beta <= c + 1 - q. It says nothing about stationary
  inverses or the dyadic norm Y. T4-145 (the T4 theory auditor, AUDITED through the T1/T3 theory worker R1-017; each application must check the realization): in
  the stated H^q realization the frozen origin gives singular sequences on the lines Re lambda = q - c - 1 and
  Re lambda = q(2c - 1) - c - 1 - a0 and on the strip between them, preserved by bounded finite-dimensional pinning
  and compact corrections; it predicts no bounded stationary inverse on a pure H^3 realization when 1 < c <= 2 and
  a0 >= 5c - 4. The exact origin slopes of the d2-warm-start candidates are above 5c_l - 4 = 5.0516: a0 = 10.31200
  (007, T4-147), 10.15404 (008, N4-105), 10.25607 (009, N4-114), 10.06231 (010, N4-122); the T4 theory auditor notes that an
  application must match the exact values and the realization before a verdict changes. No registered framework uses
  a pure H^3 realization. For B > 0 the frozen interior strip is residual spectrum (the adjoint has an
  infinite-dimensional kernel and the primal operator is bounded below with closed non-dense range), not approximate
  spectrum, as the T2 theory worker the review of T4-145 Remark R1 states (CONFIRMED by T4-171 for the natural frozen realization; no
  transfer to variable coefficients, pinning or Y is inferred). For 2 < q <= 3 the 007 to 010 candidates have
  B = 2q(c_l - 1) - a0 < 0 (a0 > 10), so this case does not arise for them. T4-146 (the T4 theory auditor, AUDITED through the T1/T3 theory worker R1-017 and a second review by the T2 theory worker, T4-164) transfers this to the actual dyadic
  inverse hypothesis (H) of FW-T-DYADIC-Y-v1: for 1 < c < 2 and a0 >= 5c - 4, (H) forces s < c - 1 and
  M >= 1/(c - 1 - s), so a necessary residual ceiling is Y(F) < H(c - 1)(1 + c - ca)/(1 + a), with H(d) the maximum
  over 0 < s < d of (d - s) sqrt(4 pi s(1 + s)) and H(c_l - 1) = 1.1302356388 < 57/50 (outward, T4-147). This
  ceiling is linear in (1 + c - ca) where the T4-097 ceiling is quadratic, and at a = 0 it is about 0.080 times
  the T4-097 ceiling (the coordinator FLOAT arithmetic). T4-147 uses it for row 64; its arithmetic is CONFIRMED (N4-128, N4-129, T4-150, R1-017), so row 64
  is RULED OUT at every 0 < a < 2. By R1-017 the ceiling of T4-146 is below the T4-097 ceiling exactly when
  beta_inf(a) > H(c_l - 1)/sqrt(8 pi) = 0.22546, that is for a < 1.4278 (FLOAT). These are necessary conditions on
  one linear estimate in one registered norm, not statements about solutions near 007. The T2 theory worker's joint ceiling
  (the review of T4-146 Remark R1, CONFIRMED by T4-165, so AUDITED): with d = c - 1 and b = 1 + c - ca > 0, using both inverse
  floors in the same realization and norm gives eta < b J(d, b)/(1 + a), J = H(d) if b >= d - s* and
  J = b sqrt(4 pi (d - b)(1 + d - b)) otherwise; it is at most both separate ceilings. No row changes. T4-148 (the T4 theory auditor) registers the scope correction of theory_v02 lines 347 to
  349: the inference that rate-one transient routes must be posed outside Y is REFUTED as an inference from the
  far-field cap beta <= 1 + c - ca, which permits rate 1 for 0 < a <= 1 (equality at a = 1) and excludes it only
  for a > 1. This proves no semigroup estimate in Y and does not change the registered L^2 theorem. T4-153 (the T4 theory auditor,
  AUDITED through the T1/T3 theory worker R1-018, accepted in T4-160) extends the H^3 obstruction of T4-145 to the weighted third-order endpoint
  with weight (1 + 4r^2)^(2/5) of the analysis2 E3 energy and any fixed positive lower-level weights: no normalized
  stationary inverse bound there when 1 < c <= 2 and a0 >= 5c - 4. analysis2 claims only growth at E3 and decay in
  weaker fractional or interpolated spaces, so this does not contradict its high inverse theorem.
- Exterior onset radii for six, seven and eight branches (the T2 theory worker handoff K, CONFIRMED by T4-063): necessary onsets that
  require an exact exterior model or a certified remainder.
- Low-to-high transfer (T4-050): a stationary L^2 inverse bound ||A_k^-1|| <= 1/2 (TM-17b, Woodbury) cannot directly
  replace (B-L2) in the T4-019 proof, because the signed low term enters the high Gram matrix (REFUTED as a direct
  substitution; the Woodbury lemma itself stands, T4-039). T4-051 replaces it by the semigroup bound (L) instead.
- No compact (finite-rank) approximation of the whole coupling Q h = U[h].grad(rho_bar) to accuracy below a_0 exists on
  L^2 or integer H^m (T4-002, CLAIMED: The T4 theory auditor self-audit); a finite-rank route must keep the principal part of Q.
- Theorem D_w (2^-35) requires |F(X)| < 2^-44 (1 + |X|^2/R^2)^(11/10) at every point and ||w F||_H < 2^-61 (section 7a).
- Critical far-field spaces (growth exponent gamma) admit no Fredholm realization, even with finitely many constraints,
  compact perturbations or tail coordinates acting compactly on the constrained core (P61, P64, P68; T4-011).
- Angular quadrature: Gauss-Legendre rules in theta with 160 or 192 nodes undercount some allowed angular modes of the
  enlarged bases (M = 59, 69) by factors between 1.5e-18 and 3.2e-12 (N4-029, reproduced by T4-024, CONFIRMED; midpoint
  exactness for N >= 2M + 2, T4-020 AUDITED). Optimization losses on under-resolved rules are not evidence of a small
  residual.
- Coefficient conversion: converting Arb values through fixed-decimal strings can lose absolute accuracy (N4-042,
  the N3 numerics worker rationalize function); use exact midpoint extraction. Exact residual bounds computed from a converted potential
  remain valid but may be loose. Rounding the C1-N1-007 coefficients to binary64 changes 421 of 2982 coefficients by at
  most 6.44e-15 and reintroduces the forbidden sector (1,0) with exponent 0.1048 and norm 7.18e-13, so the rounded
  residual is not in L^2 (N4-096, CONFIRMED by T4-117): sector and membership statements hold for the exact rational snapshot
  only. FLOAT background quantities on a truncated domain miss the exterior: for 007 the omitted exterior mass is
  0.336 percent of kappa^2 (N4-097, CONFIRMED by T4-118).
- Negative norm code of the N1/N2 numerics worker (ipm_hminus1.py, frozen sha256 1c227353...): the outer-outer Green term has prefactor
  pi/(2n) instead of pi/n (N4-067, REFUTED, exact counterexample F = r^-4 sin(theta); CONFIRMED by T4-066). The N1/N2 numerics worker
  reports the repair (23:12Z, its status file) and a FLOAT d2 value 390.2169. The exact d2 split at r = 8356.47
  (N4-072, N4-ORIGIN) puts 97.6 percent of the remaining discrepancy in the interior part. With the factor corrected the
  displayed d2 contributions give H = 390.21692, 8.3e-5 above N4's full-plane value 390.2168 (N4-020, now CONFIRMED by
  the N3 numerics worker's independent enclosure [390.2168349091, 390.2168349356]). Every the N1/N2 numerics worker
  FLOAT ||F||_H value must be recomputed with the repair. Mpen2 ||F||_H = 412.368153 (N4-068, Arb2048, VALIDATED
  N4-ORIGIN).
